                  SourceExpr=ItemFilter_G;
                  OnValidate=VAR
                               Item_L@8053000 : Record 27;
                             BEGIN
                               Item_L.GET(ItemFilter_G);
                             END;

                  OnLookup=VAR
                             Item_L@8053000 : Record 27;
                             SubFamily_L@8053003 : Record 8052987;
                           BEGIN
                             IF PAGE.RUNMODAL(PAGE::"Item List",Item_L) = ACTION::LookupOK THEN
                             BEGIN
                              SubFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Sub-Family Code");
                              IF SubFamily_L."Medicinal Food" = FALSE THEN
                               ERROR(TEXT8053000);
                               ItemFilter_G := Item_L."No.";
                             END;
                           END;
                            }

      { 8053003;2;Field     ;
                  CaptionML=FRA=Date;
                  SourceExpr=PostingDateFilter_G;
                  OnValidate=VAR
                               cduApplicationManagement_L@8053000 : Codeunit 1;
                               Item_L@8053001 : Record 27;
                             BEGIN
                               IF cduApplicationManagement_L.MakeDateFilter(PostingDateFilter_G) = 0 THEN;
                               Item_L.SETFILTER("Date Filter", PostingDateFilter_G);
                               PostingDateFilter_G := Item_L.GETFILTER("Date Filter");
                             END;
                              }

    }

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function
